**1 REAL LIFE :** Eurovision 2024 official stage design revealed
News Article: SVT, a Swedish national public television broadcaster, has revealed the stage design for the upcoming Eurovision Song Contest. The creative team, including lighting and screen content designer Fredrik Stormby and production designer Florian Wieder, has created a 360-degree experience for the live audience and viewers at home. The design features a cross-shaped stage with movable LED cubes, LED floors, and advanced lighting and video technology. A large-scale video and light installation forms the focal point, allowing the lighting design to adapt to each artist and their performance. Green Wall Designs, a company known for large-scale productions, aims to achieve lower energy consumption by utilizing LED and laser light sources exclusively. The 68th Eurovision Song Contest will culminate in the Grand Final on May 11th, 2024. News Images:
